• Anticipating another strong year into 2027 — MPI has maintained its 
current guidance for capacity plan into next year: year end-2026 capacity 
targets of VPC 2m and MEMS 3.5m, and 2027 MEMS expansion is expected 
to be at least another 2.5m. However, we see potential upside thanks to 
robust customer demand and market share gain. CPO insertion 2 is under 
final evaluation and insertion 3 already starts engineering  shipments  to 
more than 5 international customers across foundry, OSAT and fabless 
segments. The company sees strong growth momentum of probe card into 
next year and CPO equipment contribution at single digit in 2027 with more 
upside into 2028.
• We stay constructive on MPI despite recent market concerns regarding AI 
demand slowing down. The key drivers are continuing to ramp-up at 
existing AI ASIC customers, incremental contributions from new AI/non-AI 
ASIC and CPU customers, further MEMS capacity expansion, and potential 
upside from CPO and microbump probe-card technology.

2
MPI
Valuation
Our target price for MPI is set at NT$7,200, based on a target PE multiple of 52x applied to our 2027-28E EPS average. We
believe our PE target, at the high end of MPI's 3-year forward PE range, is justified given MPI’s earnings power remains
structurally higher than that in the previous cycles, broadening demand across AI ASIC, additional CPU opportunities and non-
AI ASIC wins. We expect MPI to see accelerating earnings growth ahead supported by sustainable sales growth and an

improving mix. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027E/28E P/B of 23x/15x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the MPI stock as High Risk for its share-price volatility. Key downside risks that could
prevent MPI shares from reaching our target price include 1) slowdown in AI ASIC demand; 2) slower-than-expected adoption
of MEMS probe cards; 3) intensifying competition from global and regional peers; 4) slow development of the CPO equipment

business.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

### Page 1

ab 11 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
Nan Ya Plastics
Glass fabric capacity expansion to strengthen
vertical integration
What's new
NYP announced plans to invest NT$11.5bn to add 102mn meters of glass fabric capacity
(+17% vs. existing 590mn meters capacity) in Zhangbin, Taiwan, by end-2029.
According to management, the expansion aims to meet growing demand from AI
servers, networking equipment and increasingly complex PCBs, with 60–70% of the

additional capacity allocated for internal use. Capacity will flexibly serve conventional E-
glass and AI-grade specialty glass fabric, with specialty products targeted to reach ~40%
of glass fabric revenue in 2-3 years. NYP is also expanding upstream yarn production and
adding over 500 weaving looms to support its planned glass fabric capacity expansion
by end-2029.
Implications
NYP: Glass fabric expansion to strengthen vertical integration; 1.5–2.0% EM OP

upside from 2030
We view the expansion positively, as glass fabric remains the most supply-constrained
product across NYP's CCL value chain, with the highest OPM of c40% in 3Q26 for glass
fabric. We expect further margin improvement in 4Q26, driven by continued price hikes
and a higher specialty glass fabric mix amid strong AI-related demand. Over the longer
term, the expansion should strengthen NYP's vertical integration and secure upstream
glass fabric supply for downstream CCL production. Assuming full utilization and

current ASP and margins, we estimate the 17% capacity expansion could lift EM OP by
1.5–2.0% from 2030 onwards, with further upside potential from specialty product mix
upgrades.
E-glass fabric: limited impact on external supply
NYP's planned capacity expansion of 102mn meters per year is equivalent to ~2% of
total industry capacity according to our estimates. We expect limited impact on external
E-glass supply, as NYP plans to use 60-70% of output internally, leaving only ~30-40mn

meters annually for external sales before accounting for specialty fabric allocation.
AI-grade specialty fabric: supply constraints likely to persist
We expect supply tightness to persist amid specialty yarn constraints and qualified fabric
production ramp-up. NYP's undisclosed capacity mix further limits visibility on
incremental AI-grade fabric supply.
Valuation: Maintain Buy and PT at NT$390/share
NYP is trading at 10x 27E P/E and 3.5x 27E P/B on our estimates.  Our 26/27E dividend

yields are 3.8%/6.3% at the current share price. We expect limited near-term valuation
impact from the glass fabric expansion, given its expected completion by end-2029.

Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya Plastics one of the core
companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals,
plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad laminates (CCL)

producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned
subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya
Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns
23% of Formosa Petrochemical.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nan Ya Plastics:
We base our price target for NYP on a SOTP valuation.
Key upside risks include: 1) stronger CCL pricing; 2) faster high-end CCL mix upgrades; and 3)

a faster petchem margin recovery.
Key downside risks are: 1) a CCL pricing reversal on faster supply additions or weaker
demand; 2) slower AI qualification/ramp of the company's CCL business; and 3) further
weakening chemicals/polyester businesses.
Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board:
Our price target for Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Upside risks include faster-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switches, stronger-than-

expected pricing increase, better-than-estimated consumer demand, and continued tightness
in BT substrates.
Downside risks include lower-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switch projects, lower-
than-expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansions, raw material
cost increases, intensified competition, and a lack of spec upgrades and content increase.
As for industry risks, substrate applications are dominated by PCs, but servers, artificial

intelligence and advanced computing are driving demand growth. We believe key risks to the
industry include slower-than-expected growth in servers, networking, and high performance
computing, ASP pressure, margin erosion, and lack of spec upgrades. We believe the flexible
circuit board and high-density interconnect board segments are more vulnerable to
fluctuations in the consumer product cycle and have lower visibility.
Nanya Technology:
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 164: 261112_gs_allring

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_gs_allring.pdf

Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Allen Chang 
+852-2978-2930 | 
allen.k.chang@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. 
Verena Jeng 
+852-2978-1681 | verena.jeng@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
All Ring Tech Co. (6187.TWO): CoWoS expansion to drive growth ahead; 
Oct rev on track; 3Q26 OP income in line; Buy
12 November 2026 | 7:57AM HKT
Oct revenues on track: Oct revenues came in at NT$978mn, +218% YoY but -16% 
MoM (vs. 3Q26 revenues +41% QoQ), accounting for 33% of our 4Q26E revenue 
forecast. Looking into 4Q26, we expect revenue to decline 11% QoQ, yet 24% ahead 
of our prior forecast on stronger-than-expected CoWoS related demand, while we 
lower our GM assumption to 50.6% (vs. 52.6% previously) to reflect a less favorable 
product mix and early stage shipments of new tools for panel level packaging and 
CPO. For 2027, we expect continued CoWoS capacity expansion at both foundry and 
OSAT customers, rising content value in panel level packaging and the ramp of the 
new CPO product line to drive growth, with GM set to recover as new products reach 
scale. Maintain Buy.

3Q26 operating income in line: Revenue was pre-announced. GM of 48.5% came in 
below GSe/Bloomberg consensus of 54.9%/54.3%, which we attribute to product 
mix, with a higher contribution from lower-margin automation equipment. However, 
with a larger revenue scale and a lower opex ratio of 15.6% (vs. GSe/cons. of 
18.2%/17.7%), operating income grew 39% QoQ, 2% / 1% behind GSe/cons., largely 
in line. Below the operating line, All Ring booked a non-operating loss of NT$55mn in 
3Q26 (vs. non-operating income of NT$174mn in 2Q26), primarily attributable to 
valuation losses from equity investment, partly reversing the gains booked in 2Q26, 
which led EPS (NT$8.49) to miss by 10% vs GSe.

### Page 2

CoWoS expansion and new product ramps to drive growth: We expect 4Q26E 
revenue to decline 11% QoQ, still well ahead of our prior forecast on CoWoS-related 
demand. Our forecast implies average monthly revenue of NT$983mn in 
November-December (broadly flat vs. October). On the margin front, we expect GM to 
remain subject to product mix, with new tools carrying lower margins in the initial ramp. 
Longer term, we remain positive on All Ring, and expect 2027-28E growth to be driven 
by 1) continued CoWoS capacity expansion at both foundry and OSAT customers; 2) 
panel-level packaging, which brings higher-ASP tools and additional process steps, 
lifting content value per line; 3) the ramp of its new CPO product line, spanning fiber 
coupling and optical engine inspection tools, which we expect to start contributing 
meaningfully in 2027; and 4) new customer penetration, including a US customer’s 
advanced packaging capacity.

Earnings revision: We incorporate the 3Q26 results into our model and revise our 
2026/2027/2028E EPS by +2.7%/0.0%/+1.7%, as 1) higher revenue 
(+9.4%/+1.4%/+3.6% vs. our prior forecasts) on stronger CoWoS-related demand and 2) 
a lower opex ratio on the larger revenue base are offset by 3) lower GM, reflecting the 
product mix and the initial ramp of new products, and 4) the non-operating loss in 
3Q26. Net net, we expect All Ring’s revenue to grow 87%/33%/29% YoY in 2026-28E 
(vs. 71%/44%/26% previously), with GM of 51.0%/53.1%/53.5% (vs. 
53.8%/54.2%/54.9% previously).

Valuation: We maintain our 12-month TP at NT$1,450 (implied 35x 2027E PE vs. 36% 
NI YoY of 2027-28E on avg.), still based on a target P/E multiple of 35x (unchanged; 
derived by applying the implied earnings growth uplift (per GSe) to the company’s 
historical valuation framework) applied to our FY27E (unchanged) EPS. We view CPO as 
an even stronger structural tailwind for All Ring than CoWoS, with the potential to drive 
multiple-fold earnings expansion over the next several years, further supporting both 
earnings visibility and scope for valuation re-rating. Maintain Buy.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

### Page 2

Earnings revision 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 8% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 1%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 9%/8% to reflect product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 6/6%). We now expect the GM expansion 
to be slower due to the higher server business contribution that is margin dilutive, thus 
revise down GPM by 0.2/0.2/0.2ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Valuation:  
We reiterate our Neutral rating on Inventec with a new 12m TP of NT$56 (from NT$61), 
based on 14.2x 2027E P/E (unchanged), which is the company’s average P/E for the past 
10 years, and factoring in our earnings revision.

### Page 3

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Inventec is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, servers, 
and consumer electronics. While we like Inventec’s solid position in the AI server ODM 
market, we are Neutral rated as we believe valuation already reflects near-term 
positives. We model Inventec earnings growth in 2026-28E to be driven by the 
commercial PC demand recovery and AI server ramp-up. We model AI servers to 
contribute more in 2026-28E. We see P/E valuation as fair at the current level compared 
to its peers and already reflects the solid growth in 2026-28E. Catalysts for further 
upside/downside would be its customers’ demand on AI servers and general server 
demand outlook.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$56 is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is 
the company’s average P/E for the past 10 years. 
Key upside/downside risks: (1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
(2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and (3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 176: 261113_daiwa_Gigabyte

Source PDF: all report pdf/261113_daiwa_Gigabyte.pdf

### Page 1

Summary: Gigabyte released its 3Q26 results after market hours on 13 November, with 
net income of TWD8.57bn (EPS: TWD12.07) beating expectations, while revenue of 
TWD154.7bn (+7% QoQ, +94% YoY) was largely in line despite robust server demand. 
Gross margin expanded 4.4pp QoQ to 14.0% (vs. our 9.6% estimate). Management cited 
factors such as higher value-added products and better cost control, though we believe 
VGA card price hikes were the key driver. Despite strong gross margin expansion and 
operating leverage, higher profit-sharing bonuses limited OPM expansion to 2.7pp QoQ to 
8.3%, still above our/consensus estimates of 5.8%/5.9%. We expect Gigabyte to benefit 
from solid AI server demand from neoclouds as they increase power budgets and capex, 
with neoclouds and enterprises becoming key Nvidia server buyers from Vera Rubin in 
2027. Nebius reaffirmed its 5GW contracted-power and 0.8-1GW connected-power end-
2026 targets, with >1GW annual deployments from 2027; CoreWeave raised its 2026 
capex guidance to USD37-39bn, with USD132.5bn in backlog. As neoclouds serve 
sovereign and enterprise customers lacking in-house hardware capabilities, we expect 
TAM growth for server brands including Dell, Gigabyte, Lenovo and Asustek. We reaffirm 
our AI server revenue forecasts of TWD412bn in 2026E and TWD565bn in 2027E. We 
expect the AI server mix to dilute the GM to 8.9% in 2027E and 8.4% in 2028E. We 
reiterate our Buy (1) rating with a 12M TP of TWD490, based on 15x the 1-year-forward 
diluted EPS; key downside risk is Nvidia’s business model change.

What’s the impact: 
Below are our read-throughs from Gigabyte’s 3Q26 results (released on 13 November).

• 3Q26 results first-take: profitability beat expectations. Gigabyte’s net income of 
TWD8.57bn (EPS: TWD12.07) beat our/Bloomberg consensus estimates by 32%/29%, 
respectively. Revenue (TWD154.7bn; up 7% QoQ and 94% YoY) was largely in-line with 
estimates, despite robust server demand. Nevertheless, the gross margin expanded by 
4.4pp QoQ to 14.0% in 3Q26 (exceeding our estimate of 9.6%). Management attributed 
the strength to an improved product mix, higher value-added products, strategic 
procurement, and better cost control, but we believe price hikes in VGA cards to be the 
key factor. Despite strong gross margin expansion and operating leverage, higher profit-
sharing bonus expenses caused the operating margin to only rise by 2.7pp QoQ to 8.3% 
(still beating our/street’s estimates of 5.8%/5.9%).

• Business outlook. We believe Gigabyte will ride on consistently solid AI server demand 
from the neoclouds, as key vendors continue to raise their power budget and capex. 
This aligns with our view that the neoclouds and enterprise will gradually become key

buyers of Nvidia AI servers starting from the Vera Rubin generation in 2027. Nebius 
(NBIS US, NR) reaffirmed its end-2026 contracted-power target of 5GW (up from >4GW 
currently contracted) and connected-power target of 0.8-1GW, with plans to deploy 
>1GW annually starting in 2027. Meanwhile, Coreweave (CRWV US, NR) raised its 
2026 capex guidance to USD37-39bn (from USD35-39bn) with an order backlog of 
USD132.5bn (vs. USD3.7bn revenue in 3Q26). We believe the neoclouds will become a 
key computing source for sovereign, enterprise and other corporate customers that 
cannot design and maintain the hardware themselves. As such, we expect the TAM for 
server brands like Dell (DELL US, USD371.17, Outperform [2], covered by Louis 
Miscioscia), Gigabyte, Lenovo (992 HK, NR) and Asustek (2357 TT, NR) to rise.

While management expects continued revenue growth and improving operating 
leverage in 2027, we reaffirm our AI server revenue assumptions for Gigabyte of 
TWD412bn in 2026E and TWD565bn in 2027E (vs. TWD180bn in 2025). We also 
believe the rising contribution from AI servers will be structurally margin-dilutive, despite 
potential benefits from higher value-added solutions, strategic procurement, and 
economies of scale. We therefore forecast gross margin to gradually normalise to 8.9% 
in 2027E and 8.4% in 2028E (vs. 14.0% in 3Q26).

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Gigabyte, with a 12M TP of TWD490, based on a target PER 
of 15x, applied to our 1-year-forward diluted EPS. Key downside risk: earnings impact from 
Nvidia’s business model change.

5347 世界(Vanguard)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/11/09 研 究 員：羅 金 鳳 (Sunny Lo) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
⚫ 4Q26 即使有產能瓶頸的限制下，但在 AI 需 求 強 勁 的 帶 動 下 ，
營 收 及 毛 利 率 將 進 一 步 成 長 。 
⚫ AI 的監管可能使產能在 AI 及非 AI 方 面 進 行 合 理 的 調 整，但 不
影響 AI 在 全 球 產 業 發 展 上 的 主 導 地 位，世 界 先 進 仍 會 持 續 進 置
12 吋產能，滿足客戶需求。 
⚫ VSMC 將於 1Q27 開始正式量產，產能將逐季上升，且都能維
持 90+% 的 產 能 利 用 率 ， 其 中 Interposer 將 的 營 收 貢 獻 將
>50%。

一. 產能受限，但 AI 需求強勁，4Q26 營運續強 
受惠 AI Server、 手 機 用 的 PMIC 需 求 強 勁 ， 帶 動 3Q26 整 體 晶 圓
出 貨 量 達 75.1 萬片，QoQ+5.33%，YoY+11.59%， 加 上 ASP 季
增 3%，及 有 利 的 匯 率 因 素，使 世 界 先 進 3Q26 整 體 營 收 達 157.02
億 元，QoQ+10.23%，YoY+27.15%，且 因 產 能 利 用 率 上 升、較 佳
的 產 品 組 合 及 ASP 季增 3%， 抵 銷 生 產 成 本 的 上 升 ， 使 毛 利 率 由
2Q26 的 32.32% 上 升 至 33.65% ， 稅 後 淨 利 30.99 億 元 ，
QoQ+4.18%，YoY+81.96%，單 季 稅 後 EPS 1.65 元，優 於 預 期 。

4Q26 消 費 性 產 品 需 求 疲 弱 ； 車用、 工控相 關 的 產 品 維 持 平 穩 ；
PC/NB 受 記 憶 體 價 格 上 漲 影 響，訂 單 下 修，但 AI 需 求 維 持 強 勁 ，
訂 單 能 見 度 達 4 個 月 ， 即 便 在 產 能 受 限 的 影 響 下 ， 產 能 利 用 率 仍
將 再 由 90%上 升 至>90%，帶 動 晶 圓 出 貨 量 QoQ+0～+2%，ASP 
QoQ+3～+5%，毛 利 率 將 再 上 升 至 34～36%，因 此，我 們 預 估 世
界 先 進 4Q26 營收 164.92 億 元，QoQ+5.03%，YoY+30.96%，毛
利率 35.03%， 稅 後 EPS 1.76 元。

二.AI 需求不受美國民主黨勝選影響 
就 AI 展 望 來 看 ，6 個 月 前 市 場 強 烈 看 好 到 2030 年仍 處 於 供 不 應
求，美 國 期 中 選 舉 民 主 黨 大 勝 後 60 小時，顯 示 出 許 多 政 策 需 要 進
行 調 整，其 中 AI 相 關 的 建 置 須 要 被 監 管、資 金 回 收 風 險、資 金 投
入 報 酬 率 、 電 力 不 足 等 的 擔 憂 ， 一 一 受 到 市 場 關 注 ， 世 界 先 進 認
為 市 場 可 能 會 進 行 調 整，但 AI 仍是全球產 業 發 展 所 需，機 會 還 是
不 變 ， 只 是 之 前 AI 客 戶 擔 心 拿 不 到 產 能 ， 非 AI 客 戶 憂 慮 產 能 排
擠，現 在 可 能 在 產 能 的 配 置 上 會 趨 於 合 理 化，不過 AI 仍然佔據全
球產業 主 要 的 地 位 ， 因 此 ， 世 界 先 進 的 8 吋 廠 持 續 成 長 ， 產 能 利
投資評等：買進

三.VSMC 將於 1Q27 量產，Interposer 營收占比>50% 
世 界 先 進 新 加 坡 首 座 12 吋 晶 圓 廠 VSMC 產 能 受 客 戶 合 約 承 諾 ，
第 一 期 產 能 已 全 數 被 客 戶 預 定 完 畢 ，06/2026 的 首批樣本正 式 產
出，目 前 產 能 導 入 試 產 中 ， 預估 1Q27 正 式 量 產(自 量 產 後 產 能 利
用 率 可 維 持>90%)，逐 季 爬 坡 到 產 能 滿 載，可 比 先 前 預 估 的 1H29
滿 載 提 前 達 成 。 因 先 進 封 裝 需 求 強 勁 ， 世 界 先 進 先 前 已 因 此 將
VSMC 的產能進行調配，Interposer 的 生 產 將 會 佔 有>50%的比
重，帶 動 世 界 先 進 AI 的 營 收 比 重 由 2025 年的 1～3%上 升 至 2026
年的 10+%，2027 年在 Interposer 的 加 入 下 將 繼 續 成 長 。

四. 預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 6.19/7.71 元 
2026 年 雖 然 車 用 及 工 控 需 求 已 觸 底 ， 但 AI 仍 然 是 半 導 體 成 長 的
主 旋 律，世 界 先 進 因 佈局 AI 及 資 料 中 心 相 關 的 電 源 產 品，目 前 供
不 應 求 ， 帶 動 世 界 先 進 AI 相 關 的 營 收 比 重 將 由 2025 年的 1～3%
上 升 至 10+%，及 有 利 的 價 格 環 境，因 此，我 們 預估世 界 先 進 2026
年 營 收 589.71 億 元 ，YoY+21.36%， 毛 利 率 32.79%， 稅 後 淨 利
116.33 億元，YoY+47.12%，稅後 EPS 6.19 元 。2027 年 不 受 美
國 選 舉 影 響 ，AI 相 關 的 產 品 仍 然 供 不 應 求 ，Interposer 放 量 生 產
將 進 一 步 帶 動 AI 相 關 營 收 的 貢 獻，加 上 有 利 的 價 格 環 境 下， 我們
預 估 世 界 先 進 2027 年 營 收 751.32 億 元 ，YoY+27.4%， 毛 利 率
33.76%，稅後 EPS 7.71 元。

五. 投資建議為買進，目標價 154 元 
世 界 先 進 在 AI 領 域 已 逐 漸 取 得 成 果，且 在 AI 需 求 強 勁 的 帶 動 下，
2026 年營運強 勁 成 長 ， 預 估 2027 年在 AI 的 帶 動 及 新 加 坡 12 吋
廠 進 度 優 於 預 期 ，Interposer 量 產 貢 獻 下 ， 產能利用率將維持在
90+%，且 在 有 利 的 價 格 環 境 下，有 利 獲 利 進 一 步 成 長， 因 此，投
資 建 議 為買 進 ， 目 標 價 154 元 (20X2027 年 稅 後 EPS)。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
世 界 先 進(5347)成 立於 1994 年 ， 成 立 之 初 以 DRAM 製 造 為 主 ，
1999 年台 積 公 司 協 助 下，成 功 導 入 邏 輯 產 品 代 工 技 術，並 成 為 台
積 之 代 工 伙 伴 。世界先進 2000 年 正 式 宣 佈 由 DRAM 廠 轉 型 為 晶
圓 代 工 公 司 ，07/2004 世 界 先 進 正 式 結 束 DRAM 生 產 製 造 ， 成 功
轉 型 為 百 分 之 百 的 晶 圓 代 工 公 司 。 為 擴 大 營 運 規 模 ， 後 續 陸 續 於
2007 年購 入 華 邦 電 的 8 吋 廠 ，2014 年 購 入 南 亞 科 技 的 八 吋 晶 圓

### Page 3

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

3 
廠房，01/2019 併 購 格 芯 公 司 位 於 位 於 新 加 坡 的 8 吋 晶 圓 廠 廠 房、
廠 務 設 施、機 器 設 備 以 及 MEMS 智 財 權 與 業 務，2020/01/01 已正
式 接 手 營 運 。 由 於 電 子 元 件 朝 高 頻 、 高 壓 、 高 電 流 發 展 ， 世 界 先
進 亦 開 始 佈 局 氮 化 鎵(GaN)與 碳 化 矽(SiC)產品。

二. 產業趨勢概況 
1、 佈 局 第 三 代 半 導 體 材 料 氮化鎵(GaN)  
半 導 體 第 一 代 材 料 為 矽 (Si)， 因 價 格 相 對 便 宜 ， 製 作 成 本 相 對 較
低 ， 目 前 仍 普 偏 應 用 於 各 類 電 子 產 品 ； 第 二 代 半 導 體 材 料 為 砷 化
鎵 ， 因 其 具 有 高 頻 、 低 雜 訊 、 高 效 率 仍 低 耗 電 等 特 性 ， 廣 泛 應 用
於 通 訊 類 產 品，如 手 機 的 功 率 放 大 器(AP)；由 於 電 動 車、5G 等相
關 應 用 要 求 高 功 率 、 高 電 壓 、 高 頻 的 特 性 ， 推 動 半 導 體 材 料 走 向
第 三 代 的 「 寬 能 隙 半 導 體 」 (WBG) ，即氮 化 鎵 (GaN) 與 碳 化 矽
(SiC)，然 因 製 作 難 度 較 Si 高 出 許 多，生 產 較 困 難，製 造 成 本 高 ，
目 前 仍 未 大 量 被 使 用 ， 不 過 其 高 功 率 、 高 電 壓 、 高 頻 、 散 熱 佳 及
可 使 終 端 產 品 做 得 更 小 的 特 性 將 是 未 來 各 廠 商 投 入 發 展 的 必 然 趨
勢。世界先進亦開始投入氮化鎵(GaN)與碳化矽(SiC)的 開 發 ， 雖
仍 處 於 開 發 的 初 期 ， 營 收 貢 獻 尚 不 明 顯 ， 然 我 們 認 為 此 發 展 為 長
期 趨 勢 不 變 ， 值 得 關 注 。 世 界 先 進 選 擇 製 作 良 率 較高及 應 力 影響
較 低 的 GaN on QST 的 技 術，已 準 備 量 產，將 因 應 高 中 低 端 產 品 ，
第 一 代 產 品 電壓>650V， 主 要 應 用 於 消 費 性 電 子 產 品 的 充 電 器 ，
由 於 第 三 代 半 導 體 技 術 複 雜 度 高，2024 年 出 貨 僅 數 百 片，但 2025
年年 成長 5～10 倍，目 前 以 充 電 樁 為 主，未 來 將 以 應 用 於 On board 
charger 為 目 標，但 因 電 壓 需 做 到 1200V，技 術 難 度 更 高，技 術 開
發 所 需 的 時 間 更 長；SiC 方 面，04/2026 已 完 成 試 產，目 標 客 戶 為
IDM， 預 估 2026 年 底 可 量 產 。

圖一、 全球 SiC 功 率 半 導 體 市 場 規 模 預 估

資料來源：Yole Development & 宏遠投顧彙 整 預 估

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

1、預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.76 元 
4Q26 消 費 性 產 品 需 求 疲 弱 ； 車 用 、 工 控 相 關 的 產 品 維 持 平 穩 ；
PC/NB 受 記 憶 體 價 格 上 漲 影 響，訂 單 下 修，但 AI 需 求 維 持 強 勁 ，
訂 單 能 見 度 達 4 個 月 ， 即 便 在 產 能 受 限 的 影 響 下 ， 產 能 利 用 率 仍
將 再 由 90%上 升 至>90%， 帶 動 晶 圓 出 貨 量 QoQ+0～+2%，ASP 
QoQ+3～+5%，毛 利 率 將 再 上 升 至 34～36%，因 此，我 們 預 估 世
界 先 進 4Q26 營收 164.92 億 元，QoQ+5.03%，YoY+30.96%，毛
利率 35.03%， 稅 後 EPS 1.76 元。

2、 預估 2026/2027 年 稅後 EPS 6.19/7.71 元 
2026 年 雖 然 車 用 及 工 控 需 求 已 觸 底 ， 但 AI 仍 然 是 半 導 體 成 長 的
主 旋 律，世 界 先 進 因 佈 局 AI 及 資 料 中 心 相 關 的 電 源 產 品，目 前 供
不 應 求 ， 帶 動 世 界 先 進 AI 相 關 的 營 收 比 重 將 由 2025 年的 1～3%
上 升 至 10+%，及 有 利 的 價 格 環 境，因 此，我 們 預 估 世 界 先 進 2026
年 營 收 589.71 億 元 ，YoY+21.36%， 毛 利 率 32.79%， 稅 後 淨 利
116.33 億元，YoY+47.12%，稅後 EPS 6.19 元。2027 年 不 受 美
國 選 舉 影 響 ，AI 相 關 的 產 品 仍 然 供 不 應 求 ，Interposer 放 量 生 產
將 進 一 步 帶 動 AI 相 關 營 收 的 貢 獻，加 上 有 利 的 價 格 環 境 下，我 們
預 估 世 界 先 進 2027 年 營 收 751.32 億 元 ，YoY+27.4%， 毛 利 率
33.76%，稅後 EPS 7.71 元。

▶Analyst 박세연 shannon@hanwha.com 3772-7406

[주요 데이터 및 차트]

NPS 낸드 사업 반기 실적  
→ 순이익 0.13 조원에서 5.84 조원으로

주: NPS는 솔리다임의 모회사  
자료: DART, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

공모 구조별 SK 하이닉스 몫 
→ 거래 직후 가치 같음(가정) 
구분 신주 100% 구주매출 100% 
SK 하이닉스 몫 약 129 조원 약 116 조원 + 현금 13.3조원 
현금이 남는 곳 솔리다임 NPS → SK 하이닉스 
전액 환원 가정 
주당 
공모 현금은 
솔리다임에 
약 1만 8,300원 
(종가의 1.3%)

주: 공모 전 지분가치 1,000억달러·조달 100억달러 가정 
자료: 블룸버그, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

솔리다임 상장이 SK 하이닉스 주주에게 이익이 될지는 상장 여부가 아
니라 조건이 정합니다. 보도된 기업가치 1,000 억달러(블룸버그)는 약 
136조원으로 SK 하이닉스 시가총액의 13% 규모입니다. 확인할 조건은 
세 가지, 상장 전 출자 가격과 공모 자금의 쓰임, 환원 계획입니다. 신
주라면 미국 투자의 수익을, 구주매출이라면 매각 대금이 주주에게 돌
아오는 길을 봐야 합니다. 판단 시점은 금융위원회 유권해석과 10 월 
28 일 실적발표, 12 월 3 일 재공시입니다.

다롄 생산기지, 미국 공장 검토 
미국은 다롄 공장(옛 인텔 다롄)을 장비 반입 포괄 허가 명단에서 빼고 
연간 허가로 전환(BIS, 2025). SK하이닉스 매출의 68.8%는 미국 판매
법인에서 발생. 솔리다임은 미국 동부에 낸드 공장 검토(Reuters, 
2026.9). 세계 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2030년 2.3배(2024년 대비)로 
늘 전망이라 대용량 SSD 수요가 커지는 것도 배경(IEA, 2025)

해외 상장에도 중복상장 규율, 이사회 의무가 관건 
금감원장은 금융위·금감원 모두 중복상장에 해당한다고 본다고 답변
(10.19 국정감사). 국내 증권신고서 대상 여부는 금융위 유권해석 대
기. 해외 상장이라 거래소 특례심사는 받지 않고 주주동의는 권고 사
항. SK㈜·SK이노베이션·SK텔레콤은 NPS 신주 인수에 총 11.1억달러를 
약정. 사업 경쟁력과 자금 조달의 필요성, 기존 주주가치에 미치는 영
향을 함께 살펴볼 필요[그림7·12]

자금의 쓰임이 관건, 환원 가정 시 주당 약 1만8,300원 
공모가가 공정하다는 가정 아래, 거래 직후 SK하이닉스의 지분가치와 
현금 합계는 신주·구주 모두 약 129조원으로 같고 현금이 남는 곳만 
다름. 구주매출 100억달러 중 NPS를 거쳐 SK하이닉스에 귀속되는 현
금을 전액 배당하면 주당 약 1만8,300원. 10월 13일 종가의 1.3%, 
2025 사업연도 주당 배당의 약 6배(회사의 배당 계획이 아닌 가정 계
산). 신주 발행으로 조달한 자금을 투자하면 주주가치는 투자수익에 달
림[표4].

2 
 I. 시장이 묻는 세 가지

1. 세 가지 질문과 확인 결과

솔리다임 상장, 
시장의 세 가지 질문 
 솔리다임 상장 보도(Bloomberg, 10/7) 뒤 시선은 SK하이닉스 주주가치로 향했다. 시장
의 질문은 세 가지다. 왜 미국 상장인가(II·III장), 상장 전 지분은 누구에게 가는가(IV
장), 주주에게 돌아올 몫은 얼마인가(V장). 
당국은 중복상장 
규율 적용 입장 
금감원장 · 10.19 국정감사 
 금감원장은 10월 19일 국정감사에서 금융위원회와 금융감독원 모두 솔리다임 상장을 
중복상장에 해당한다고 본다고 답했다. 다만 해외 상장이라 거래소 특례심사는 받지 않
고, 주주동의가 필수인 물적분할 자회사도 아니다. 표4 가정(추가 프리IPO 제외)대로면 
SK하이닉스의 솔리다임 실질 지분은 약 85~86%다(6월 말 95.08%). 국내 증권신고서 
대상인지는 금융위원회 유권해석을 기다리고 있다. 
미국 공시 체계, 
모회사는 이미 편입 
 미국 상장으로 엄격한 공시 규율을 따르면 기업가치를 높게 평가받을 수 있다는 것이 
자기구속(bonding) 가설이다(Coffee, 2002). 미국에 교차상장한 외국 기업의 토빈 q는 
같은 나라의 미국 미상장 기업보다 16.5% 높았다는 연구도 있다(Doidge 외, 2004, 
1997년 표본).  
Coffee, J. C., “Racing Towards the Top?: The Impact of Cross-Listings and Stock Market Competition on International Corporate 
Governance”, Columbia Law Review 102(7)

SK하이닉스는 7월 10일 나스닥 ADR 상장으로 이미 미국 공시 체계에 편입됐다. 솔리
다임 상장의 추가 효과는 자금 조달과 투자자 접근성 측면에서 따로 평가해야 한다. 
삼성전자와 차이 
1.2%p 이내 
 보도일과 다음 거래일 이틀 동안 SK하이닉스는 프리IPO 보도 때 5.2%, 주관사 선정 
때 6.8% 내렸다. 삼성전자(4.0%, 6.7%)보다 각각 1.2%p, 0.1%p 더 내렸다. 두 구간 모
두 SK하이닉스는 삼성전자와 비슷하게 움직였다(한 종목과 견준 이틀 관찰)[그림1]. 
10월 13일 낙폭은 
솔리다임 공시 없던 날 
 10월 13일 코스피는 5.46% 내린 6,035.35에 마감했고, SK하이닉스는 10.09%, 삼성전
자는 7.39% 내렸다. 이날 솔리다임 관련 공시는 없었다. SK하이닉스는 삼성전자보다 
2.7%p 더 내렸지만, 이 비교로는 그 원인을 가려낼 수 없다. 
[그림1] 솔리다임 보도 전후 이틀 주가 → 두 보도 구간 모두 삼성전자와 차이는 1.2%p 이내

주: 보도 전 거래일 종가 대비 다음 거래일 종가(10/9 휴장)  
자료: KRX, 언론 보도, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

3

2. 연구가 본 중복상장 할인의 조건

연구가 본 할인 요인, 
가치 이전 위험 
선행 연구 
 중복상장 할인의 근거로는 흔히 지배주주와 일반주주 사이의 이해상충이 꼽힌다. 대표
적인 예가 지배주주를 위해 회사의 자산과 이익을 옮기는 터널링이다(Johnson 외, 
2000). 지배권이 현금흐름권을 웃돌수록 기업가치는 낮았다(Claessens 외, 2002). 한국 
기업집단을 다룬 연구도 계열사 간 인수와 사모 발행이 소수주주 몫에 영향을 준다는 
점을 보여 줬다(Bae·Kang·Kim, 2002; Baek·Kang·Lee, 2006)[표1]. 그래서 시장은 계열
사 거래의 가격 산정 근거를 눈여겨본다. 
자금의 쓰임 따라 
모회사 반응도 달라 
 자회사 상장이 늘 악재는 아니다. 미국에서는 자회사 공모 발표 때 모회사 주가가 오르
는 경향이 있었다(Schipper·Smith, 1986). 공모 자금을 주로 부채 상환·배당에 쓰겠다고 
밝힌 표본의 누적초과수익률은 +6.63%, 투자 목적으로 보유하겠다고 밝힌 표본은 
−0.01%였다(Allen·McConnell, 1998). 할인 여부는 상장 자체보다 공모 구조와 자금의 
쓰임에 달려 있다. 
같은 상장도 
분위기 따라 달리 읽혀 
 같은 중복상장도 시장 분위기에 따라 다르게 읽힌다. 미국 기술주 거품기에는 자회사 
주식이 비싸게 거래돼, 모회사 시가총액이 보유 자회사 지분가치보다 낮아지는 가격 오
류까지 나타났다. 공매도가 어려워 이 오류는 한동안 풀리지 않았다(Lamont·Thaler, 
2003). 심리가 높았던 시기 뒤에는 가치를 매기기 어려운 주식의 수익률이 상대적으로 
낮았다(Baker·Wurgler, 2006). 솔리다임 보도일과 다음 거래일 이틀 동안 SK하이닉스 
주가는 8월과 10월 두 구간 모두 삼성전자와 비슷하게 움직였다[그림1].

[표1] 중복상장 관련 주요 연구 → 가치 이전 위험이 할인 요인으로 꼽히고, 자금을 회사 밖에 쓰겠다고 밝힌 표본의 반응이 더 좋음 
연구 핵심 발견 인용 수 
Johnson 외(2000, AER) 지배주주를 위해 회사의 자산과 이익을 옮기는 「터널링」 개념 정리 2,211 
Claessens 외(2002, JF) 동아시아 기업에서 지배권이 현금흐름권을 웃돌수록 기업가치 하락 3,842 
Bae·Kang·Kim(2002, JF) 한국 기업집단 계열사 인수에서 인수회사 주가는 하락, 지배주주는 다른 계열사 가치 상승으로 이득 1,170 
Baek·Kang·Lee(2006, JF) 한국 재벌 계열사의 사모 증권 발행에서 발행가를 통해 지배주주 쪽으로 가치 이전 576 
Schipper·Smith(1986, JFE) 자회사 공모 발표 때 모회사 주가 상승(같은 회사의 유상증자 발표와 반대) 362 
Allen·McConnell(1998, JF) 공모 자금을 주로 부채 상환·배당에 쓰겠다고 밝힌 표본 누적초과수익률 +6.63%, 투자 목적 보유 표본 −0.01% 174 
Lamont·Thaler(2003, JPE) 미국 기술주 자회사 상장에서 모회사 시가총액이 보유 자회사 지분가치보다 낮은 가격 오류가 공매도 제약으로 지속 660 
Doidge 외(2004, JFE) 미국에 교차상장한 외국 기업의 토빈 q 가 같은 나라 기업보다 16.5% 높음(1997 년 표본) 1,627 
Baker·Wurgler(2006, JF) 투자 심리가 높았던 시기 뒤에는 가치평가·차익거래가 어려운 주식의 수익률이 상대적으로 낮음 6,011

주: 인용 수는 OpenAlex 기준(2026년 10월 14일 조회) 
자료: 각 논문, OpenAlex, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

### Page 4

ESG [한화리서치]

4 
 II. 지정학: 다롄 공장과 미국 거점

1. 미·중 승인 아래 놓인 다롄, 미국 거점을 검토하는 솔리다임

낸드 생산 거점, 
중국 다롄 
인수 대금 88억달러 
 솔리다임 낸드는 인텔에서 넘겨받은 중국 랴오닝성 다롄 공장에서 만든다. SK하이닉스
는 인텔 낸드 사업 인수에 2021년 12월 66억달러, 2025년 3월 22억달러를 치렀다(SK
하이닉스 F-1/A, 2026). 다롄은 192단 제품을 만든다(Blocks & Files, 2026.9.18). 
장비 반입은 
해마다 허가 
미국 상무부 · 2025.8.29 
 미국 상무부는 2025년 8월 29일 장비 반입 포괄 허가(VEU) 명단에서 다롄 공장(옛 
Intel Semiconductor (Dalian))과 SK하이닉스 중국 법인을 빼기로 했다. 고시의 시행일
은 2025년 12월 31일이다(BIS, 2025)[그림4]. 대신 해마다 필요한 미국산 장비 계획을 
내고 승인받는 방식으로 바꿨고, 회사는 2025년 12월 2026년분 연간 허가를 받았다
(SK하이닉스 F-1/A, 2026)[그림2]. 생산은 이어지지만, 장비 반입은 해마다 미국의 허
가를 받아야 한다. 
중국 쪽 조건도 
12월 이후 해제 신청 
 중국 시장감독관리총국(SAMR)은 인텔 낸드 인수를 승인하며 2021년 12월부터 5년간 
기업용 SSD(PCIe·SATA)의 중국 판매가 상한, 생산 확대, 경쟁사 진입 지원 등을 조건
으로 붙였다. 판매가는 거래 조건이 같으면 승인 전 24개월 평균을 넘지 못한다
(SAMR, 2021). 회사는 이 조건 때문에 2026년 중국에 파는 낸드 제품 값을 크게 올리
기 어렵다고 밝혔다. 5년이 지나면 해제를 신청할 수 있고, 승인 여부는 당국이 정한다
(SK하이닉스 F-1/A, 2026)[그림2]. 다롄은 미국의 장비 반입 허가와 중국의 가격·생산 
조건, 두 가지 틀 안에서 운영된다.

[그림2] SK 하이닉스 F-1/A 원문 → 장비 반입은 해마다 허가, 중국 판매가는 5 년 조건, 2025 년 매출의 68.8%는 미국 판매법인

자료: SEC, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

### Page 5

ESG [한화리서치]

5 
 2. 미국 공장 검토와 남은 일정

미국 공장 검토, 
공모 자금이 재원 후보 
 솔리다임은 미국 동부(뉴욕주가 후보로 거론)에 낸드 공장을 짓는 방안을 검토하고 있
다(Reuters, 2026.9). 미국 공장에는 다롄에 들이기 어려운 최신 장비를 들일 수 있다. 
신주로 조달한 공모 자금은 그 건설 재원이 될 수 있다. 솔리다임 낸드 공장과는 별개
로, SK하이닉스는 미국 인디애나에 첨단 패키징 공장을 짓고 있다. 2028년 10월 클린룸
을 열고 2029년 하반기 양산할 계획이다(착공식 발표, 2026.8.27). 이 사업은 요건을 채
우면 미국 정부 보조금 최대 4.58억달러와 대출 최대 5.7억달러를 받을 수 있다(SK하이
닉스 F-1/A, 2026). 미·중 승인과 상장 일정은 [그림3]에 모았다.

[그림3] 솔리다임과 다롄 공장을 둘러싼 일정 → 다롄은 미·중 두 조건 아래, 미국 공장은 생산 거점 분산·상장은 자금 조달의 선택지

주: VEU 제외는 BIS(2025), 인수 대금·중국 조건·연간 허가는 SK하이닉스 F-1/A(2026), 나머지는 DART 공시와 언론 보도 기준. 당사 투자의견과 무관 
자료: SEC, DART, Bloomberg, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

[그림4] 미국 연방관보 원문: VEU 명단 제외 → 다롄 공장(옛 인텔 다롄)이 이름으로 명시, 2025 년 12 월 31 일 시행

주: 미국 연방관보 90 FR 42321(2025.9.2) 
자료: 미국 연방관보(BIS), 한화투자증권 리서치센터

6 
 III. 에너지 전환: 전력이 고르는 저장장치

1. 데이터센터 전력 제약과 대용량 SSD

전력이 모자라면 
용량당 전력이 관건 
 국제에너지기구(IEA)는 세계 데이터센터 전력 사용이 2024년 415TWh에서 2030년 
945TWh로 늘 것으로 본다[그림5]. 2024년 사용량의 45%가 미국이다. 전력이 제약이 
되면 같은 전력에 더 많이 담는 저장장치가 유리하다. 솔리다임은 122TB QLC(셀당 4
비트 낸드) SSD로 저장장치를 꾸리면 HDD·SSD 혼용 구성보다 설치 공간은 최대 4분
의 1, 저장장치 전력은 최대 84% 준다고 밝혔다(Solidigm, 2024). 
미국 공장은 공급망, 
상장은 자금 조달 
 NPS의 낸드 사업 반기 매출은 1년 새 3.7배가 됐다[그림9]. SK하이닉스 전체 매출의 
68.8%가 미국 판매법인에서 나왔다(2025년; SK하이닉스 F-1/A, 2026)[그림2]. 데이터
센터 전력 증가도 미국이 이끌었다[그림6]. 미국 공장은 공급망을, 미국 상장은 자금 조
달을 넓히는 수단이 될 수 있다.

[그림5] 세계 데이터센터 전력 사용 → 2030 년 945TWh 로 2024 년의 2.3 배, 2024 년 사용량의 45%가 미국

자료: IEA, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

[그림6] IEA 원문: 지역별 데이터센터 전력 사용 → 2017 년 이후 증가는 미국이 주도

자료: IEA, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

7 
 IV. 지배구조: 상장 전 지분

1. 지분 사슬과 계열사 출자

계열사의 
NPS 출자 약정 
약정 합계 11.1억달러 
 솔리다임은 SK하이닉스의 미국 법인 NPS(SK hynix NAND Product Solutions)가 지분 
96.86%를 가진 회사다(6월 말). 올해 SK㈜(2.5억달러), SK이노베이션(미국 법인 경유 
3.8억달러), SK텔레콤(4.8억달러, 4년 분할)이 NPS 신주를 받기로 약정했다[표2]. NPS는 
외국법인이어서 국내 계열회사 주식 소유 제한(공정거래법 제18조)의 대상이 아니다. 
직접 지분의 가치는 
SK㈜ 몫 
 SK㈜는 SK스퀘어 지분 32.2%를, SK스퀘어는 SK하이닉스 지분 20.0%를, SK하이닉스
는 NPS 지분 98.16%를 갖고 있어 지분율을 곱한 SK㈜의 NPS 간접 지분은 약 6.3%
다. 여기에 직접 지분 1.27%가 더해졌다(6월 말). SK㈜는 별도·연결 재무제표 모두에서 
NPS를 관계기업으로 처리한다. 상장에 따른 시장가치 변화가 장부에 바로 잡히지는 않
지만, 직접 지분의 가치 변동은 지분을 가진 SK㈜에 귀속된다[그림7].

[그림7] SK 그룹 지분도와 솔리다임 상장 사슬 → 지주회사·자회사·손자회사가 모두 상장된 사슬 아래, 계열사가 NPS 에 직접 출자

주: 지분율은 2026년 6월 말 기준(SK스퀘어의 SK하이닉스 지분은 7월 ADR 신주 상장 뒤 주식 수 기준) 
자료: DART, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

[표2] 공시 원문 발췌: 계열사의 NPS 출자 → 세 회사 약정 11.1 억달러, SK텔레콤은 642 주를 3,971 억원에 취득하기로 결정 
공시 원문 발췌 
SK 텔레콤(2026.6.29, 7.23 정정공시) 발행회사 SK hynix NAND Product Solutions Corp.(미국). 취득주식수 642 주, 취득금액 397,129,047,957원, 취득 후 지분 0.62%. 현금출자, 취득예정일 2026-07-31(7.23 정정) 
같은 공시: 취득목적 「당사 AI 사업과의 시너지 확보 측면에서, SK hynix NAND Product Solutions Corp.의 지분 확보 추진」 
SK㈜(2026.8.14) 「총 약정금액은 USD 250,000 천이며, 당반기말 현재 USD 133,891천의 출자가 완료되었습니다」. 같은 주석의 SK 텔레콤 약정: 「총 약정금액은 USD 480,000 천이며, … 2026 년 6월 25 일부터 4 년간」 
SK 이노베이션(2026.8.14) 「2026 년 7 월 23 일자로 관계기업인 SK hynix NAND Product Solutions Corp.에 USD 143.4백만을 추가 출자하였습니다」. 약정 총액 USD 380,000 천(SK Innovation America 경유)

자료: DART, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

8 
 2. 출자 단가와 실적

기준이 다른 
세 가치 지표 
 SK㈜ 반기보고서의 NPS 장부가와 지분율로 역산한 장부가 기준 NPS 가치는 약 16조
원이다. SK텔레콤의 취득 단가(642주, 3,971억원)에 신주 발행 전 주식 수(101,875주)를 
곱하면 약 63조원이다. 장부가 역산과 거래 단가는 산출 기준이 달라, 두 값의 차이만으
로 출자 가격의 적정성을 판단할 수는 없다. 낸드 사업 반기 순이익은 지난해 상반기 
0.13조원에서 올해 상반기 5.84조원으로 늘었다[그림9]. 보도된 상장 기업가치(약 136
조원)는 NPS가 아니라 솔리다임 기준 값이다[그림8]. 
출자 단가는 
계열사 공시로 확인 
 출자 단가는 신주를 받은 계열사의 공시와 주석에서 확인할 수 있다[표2]. SK텔레콤 약
정 잔여분(4년 분할)과 외부 프리IPO가 어떤 단가와 규모로 이뤄지는지가 상장 전 SK
하이닉스 주주의 몫을 정할 것이다.

[그림8] 기준이 다른 세 가치 지표(NPS·솔리다임) → 평가 대상·산출 방식이 달라 직접 비교에는 한계

주: 5~6월은 SK㈜ 별도 장부가(1,997억원) ÷ 지분율(1.27%)로 역산값. 세 값은 산출 기준이 달라 직접 비교에 한계. 
자료: DART, 블룸버그, 언론 보도, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

[그림9] NPS 낸드 사업 반기 실적 → 매출 3.7 배, 순이익 0.13 조원에서 5.84 조원으로

주: SK하이닉스 반기보고서 연결 주석의 NPS 요약재무 
자료: DART, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

9 
 3. 솔리다임의 비중

기업가치로는 
시가총액의 약 13% 
10월 13일 종가 기준 
 NPS 낸드 사업이 SK하이닉스 연결에서 차지하는 몫은 상반기 매출의 9.3%, 순이익의 
4.3%, 자산의 5.7%, 순자산의 3.1%다. 순이익 비중이 낮은 것은 연결 순이익에 금융상
품 평가이익 등이 반영됐기 때문이다. 보도된 기업가치(약 136조원)는 10월 13일 SK하
이닉스 시가총액(1,054조원)의 약 13%다(회계 비중과는 기준이 다름)[그림10]. 
회사 입장은 
확정된 사항 없음

SK하이닉스는 8월 5일과 9월 4일 보도 관련 공시에서 솔리다임이 경쟁력 강화를 위해 
다양한 방안을 검토하고 있으나 확정된 사항은 없다고 밝혔다. 다음 재공시 예정일은 
12월 3일이다. SK텔레콤의 NPS 취득 공시와 SK하이닉스의 보도 관련 공시 원문은 [그
림11]에 실었다.

[그림10] SK 하이닉스 안의 NPS(낸드) 비중 → 회계 숫자로는 3~9%, 보도된 기업가치로는 시가총액의 약 13%

주: NPS 낸드 사업 ÷ SK하이닉스 연결(2026년 6월 말). 연결 순이익에는 금융상품 평가이익 등 금융수익(상반기 82.9조원)이 반영. 기업가치 136조원은 1,000억달러 × 1,360원(SBS 
Biz, 2026.10.8 환산) 
자료: DART, KRX, 블룸버그, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

[그림11] 공시 원문: SK 텔레콤의 NPS 취득과 SK 하이닉스의 보도 관련 공시 → 642 주 3,971 억원 취득 결정, 상장은 「확정된 사항 없음」

자료: DART, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

10 
 4. 해외 상장에 적용되는 규율

8월부터 
엄격한 특례심사 
거래소 규정 · 8월 3일 
 금융위원회는 7월 31일 거래소 상장·공시 규정 개정을 의결해 8월 3일부터 중복상장을 
엄격한 특례심사 대상으로 바꿨다. 일반주주 권익을 고려하지 않는 비대칭적 중복상장
은 엄격히 금지한다는 취지다. 모회사 이사회는 독립 특별위원회 심의를 거쳐 주주영향
평가, 주주보호 방안, 주주 소통·동의 확인, 찬반 결의, 공시라는 다섯 가지 의무를 진다. 
금융위원회는 이 의무를 자회사 해외상장 때에도 적용한다고 밝혔다(금융위, 
2026.7.31)[그림12]. 
거래소 심사 미적용, 
당국은 규율 적용 입장 
 해외 상장은 거래소의 중복상장 특례심사를 거치지 않는다. 다만 금감원장은 금융위·금
감원 모두 중복상장에 해당한다고 본다고 답했다(10.19 국정감사). 주주동의가 필수인 
곳은 물적분할 자회사뿐이고, 솔리다임 같은 일반 자회사는 권고 대상이다(금융위, 
2026.7.31). 국회에는 자회사 상장 때 모회사 주총 특별결의를 받게 하는 자본시장법 개
정안(안도걸 의원 등 14인, 9월 9일 발의)이 정무위원회에 있다[표3].

[그림12] 금융위원회 중복상장 제도개선 구조도(원문) → 모회사 이사회 5 대 의무는 자회사 해외상장 때에도 적용

자료: 금융위원회, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

[표3] 솔리다임 상장에 적용되는 규율 → 거래소 심사는 미적용이나 당국은 규율 적용 입장, 이사회 의무가 핵심 
규율 내용 솔리다임 적용 
공정거래법 제18 조 지주회사는 자회사 외, 자회사는 손자회사 외 국내 계열회사 주식 소유 금지. 손자회사는 국내 계열회사 주식을 100% 가질 때만 소유 대상 아님(외국법인) 
거래소 중복상장 특례심사 자회사의 독립성, 이사회 5 대 의무 이행과 찬성 결의, 주주동의 여부 심사 미적용(미국 상장) 
모회사 이사회 5 대 의무 주주영향평가, 주주보호 방안, 주주 소통·동의 확인, 특별위원회 심의 뒤 찬반 결의, 단계별 공시 적용(금융위원회) 
주주동의 물적분할 자회사는 필수, 그 밖의 자회사는 권고. 3% 초과 의결권 제한, 출석 과반·전체 4분의 1 이상 권고(일반 자회사) 
자본시장법 개정안 자회사 상장 때 모회사 주총 특별결의(의안 2221240, 9 월 9일 발의; SBS Biz, 2026.10.8) 정무위원회 계류

주: 중복상장 규정은 2026.7.31 의결, 8.3 시행. 해외 상장 적용은 금융위원회 보도참고자료(2026.7.31) 기준 
자료: 국가법령정보센터, 금융위원회, 국회 의안정보시스템, 언론 보도, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

### Page 11

ESG [한화리서치]

11 
 V. 주주가치: 공모 구조와 자금의 쓰임

1. 공모 구조별 SK 하이닉스 주주 손익

거래 직후 기준, 
신주든 구주든 가치 중립 
 공모가가 공정하면 신주 발행이든 구주매출이든 SK하이닉스 몫(지분가치와 현금의 합)
은 같다. 차이는 현금이 어디에 남느냐다. 신주를 내면 현금은 솔리다임에 남아 미국 공
장 등 투자 재원이 될 수 있고, 구주를 팔면 대금은 먼저 NPS에 들어오고, NPS의 배당
을 거쳐야 SK하이닉스의 환원 재원이 될 수 있다[표4]. 
전액 환원 가정 시 
주당 약 1만8,300원 
가정 · 100억달러 
 보도된 규모인 100억달러(약 13.6조원)를 구주매출로 조달하고, NPS가 이를 배당해 SK
하이닉스가 받은 현금(NPS 지분 98.16%분)을 다시 전액 특별배당한다고 가정하면 주
당 약 1만8,300원이다. 10월 13일 종가의 1.3%, 2025 사업연도 주당 배당(3,000원)의 6
배다. 다만 8월에 결정한 소각용 자사주 매입(40조원)과 견주면 3분의 1 규모다. 회사의 
배당 계획이 아니라 규모를 가늠하기 위한 가정 계산이다. 
추가 출자의 
가격과 조건 
 공모 전 외부 지분이 어떤 값과 조건으로 정해지는지도 SK하이닉스 주주의 몫을 좌우
한다. 출자 이후의 가치 상승분은 그 지분을 가진 계열사에 돌아간다. 다만 과거 출자 
단가가 상장 때의 기업가치보다 낮았다는 것만으로 기존 주주에게 불리한 거래였다고 
볼 수는 없다. 남은 출자와 프리IPO의 단가가 공정가에 가까울수록 SK하이닉스 주주의 
몫은 지켜진다. 
공모 구조의 평가, 
쓰임과 투자수익이 결정 
실적 개선 뒤의 값 
 보도된 기업가치(약 136조원)는 최근 실적 개선 뒤에 나온 값이다[그림8]. 이 값이 적정
한지는 이익이 이어지는지에 달려 있다. 구주매출은 판 지분만큼 지금의 평가를 현금으
로 바꾸는 길이고, 신주 발행은 지분을 일부 내주는 대신 미국 투자 재원을 솔리다임에 
넣어 수익으로 돌려받는 길이다. 신주로 조달한 자금을 미국 공장에 쓴다면, 주주가치는 
투자수익과 지정학적 위험을 줄이는 효과에 달려 있다. 어떤 공시가 나오면 무엇을 볼
지는 [표7]에 정리했다.

[표4] 공모 구조별 SK 하이닉스 몫(가정) → 공정가면 지분가치와 현금의 합은 같고, 현금이 남는 곳만 다름 
구분 신주 발행 100% 구주매출 100% 
조달액(가정) 100 억달러(약 13.6 조원) 같음 
현금이 남는 곳 솔리다임(미국 공장 재원 가능) NPS·SK 하이닉스(환원 재원 가능) 
SK 하이닉스 실질 지분(6 월 말 95.08%) 약 86.4% 약 85.3% 
SK 하이닉스 몫(보도 기업가치 기준) 약 129 조원 약 116 조원 + 현금 13.3 조원 
전액 환원 가정 시 주당 공모 현금은 솔리다임에 약 1만 8,300원(10/13 종가의 1.3%)

주: 1,000억달러를 공모 전 지분가치로 보고 100억달러를 조달한다고 둔 가정 계산. 거래 직후의 산술 비교로 주가 영향·투자수익·세금은 반영하지 않음. 당사 투자의견과 무관 
자료: DART, 블룸버그, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

### Page 12

ESG [한화리서치]

12 
 2. 종목별 영향과 판단 변수

종목마다 
받는 영향이 달라 
 솔리다임 상장이 SK그룹 상장사에 미치는 영향은 종목마다 다르다. SK하이닉스의 솔리
다임 실질 지분은 6월 말 공시 기준 95.08%로, 공모 구조·출자 가격·자금의 쓰임이 주
요 변수다. SK스퀘어는 SK하이닉스 지분을 통해 간접적으로 영향을 받는다. SK하이닉
스의 환원 정책과 보유 자산가치가 함께 반영된다. SK㈜·SK텔레콤·SK이노베이션은 
NPS 직접 지분의 가치 변화와 남은 출자 단가를 함께 봐야 한다[표5]. 
상장은 낸드 사업의 
가격표 
 솔리다임 상장은 SK하이닉스 연결 안에 있던 낸드 사업에 시장 가격을 따로 붙인다. 시
가총액의 약 13%로 보도된 기업가치가 독립된 값으로 평가받는 계기다[그림10]. 회사
는 8월에 40조원 규모의 소각용 자사주 매입을 결정해 환원 원칙을 이미 보여 줬다. 시
장이 확인하려는 것은 구주매출로 현금이 생길 때 이 원칙이 이어지는지다. 변동성이 
큰 시기에는 주주보호 방안을 먼저 공개할수록 시장의 신뢰를 빨리 얻을 수 있다. 
판단을 바꿀 
변수 네 가지 
 이 판단을 바꿀 변수는 네 가지다. 상장 시점은 메모리 업황과 미국 기술주 공모 시장의 
분위기에 달려 있다. 미국의 2027년분 연간 허가는 다롄 장비 반입을 정한다. 중국 당
국의 조건 해제는 중국 판매가와 생산 의무를 바꾼다. 미국 공장은 투자 규모와 보조금 
조건이 확정돼야 수익성을 따질 수 있다. 
해외 상장, 
다른 그룹의 선례 
 해외 상장에는 거래소 심사가 적용되지 않지만, 당국은 중복상장 규율을 적용한다는 입
장이다. 금융위원회의 유권해석은 해외 자회사 상장 전반의 선례가 된다. 현대차(2024
년 10월)와 LG전자(2025년 10월)는 인도 법인을 현지에 상장하며 보유 주식을 파는 
구주매출을 택했다. 국회 법안이 대통령령으로 해외 증시까지 넓히면 모회사 주총 특별
결의가 해외 상장에도 걸린다(SBS Biz, 2026.10.8). 해외 자회사 상장을 검토하는 그룹은 
이 법안의 진행을 함께 봐야 한다.

[표5] SK 그룹 상장사별 솔리다임 노출과 판단 조건 → 같은 상장이라도 종목마다 봐야 할 조건이 다름 
종목 지분 보유 경로 영향 경로 주요 판단 조건 
SK 하이닉스 NPS 98.16% × 솔리다임 96.86%(실질 95.08%) 신주면 지분 희석·현금은 솔리다임에, 구주매출이면 NPS에 현금 유입. 주가는 메모리 업황이 주변수 공모가의 적정성, 신주·구주 비율, 조달 자금의 투자수익과 환원, 남은 출자 단가 
SK 스퀘어 SK 하이닉스 지분 20.0%를 거친 간접 노출 보유 자산가치와 SK 하이닉스 환원 정책이 함께 반영 SK 하이닉스의 환원 정책, 순자산가치 대비 할인율  
SK㈜ NPS 직접 1.27%(6월 말), SK 스퀘어·SK 하이닉스를 통한 간접 보유 직접 지분의 경제적 가치가 SK㈜에 귀속 추가 출자의 단가·권리조건과 희석 영향, 총약정 2.5 억달러 중 남은 납입액 
SK 텔레콤 NPS 0.62%(642 주, 취득 결정 기준) 직접 지분의 가치 변화, 4 년 분할 출자 추가 출자의 단가·권리조건과 희석 영향, 총약정 4.8 억달러 중 남은 납입액 
SK 이노베이션 미국 법인 경유 NPS 출자(약정 3.8 억달러) 미국 법인이 보유한 NPS 지분의 가치 변화와 추가 출자 부담 추가 출자의 단가·권리조건과 희석 영향,  잔여 약정의 납입 부담과 출자 재원

주: 지분은 2026년 6월 말 공시 기준(SK스퀘어는 7월 ADR 뒤, SK텔레콤은 취득 결정 기준). 노출 경로 정리로 투자의견·순위 아님 
자료: DART, 한화투자증권 리서치센터

### Page 13

ESG [한화리서치]

13 
 3. 확인할 공시와 결론

상장 사슬이 
네 단계로 
 SK㈜·SK스퀘어·SK하이닉스는 이미 모두 상장돼 있다. 솔리다임까지 상장하면 SK㈜에
서 솔리다임으로 이어지는 수직 사슬에 상장사가 네 곳 놓인다. 상장사가 늘어날수록 
각 단계의 주주에게 가치와 현금이 어떻게 전달되는지를 설명하는 일이 중요해진다. 
보호 방안의 
내용과 이행 
 가이드라인은 주주보호 방안의 예로 자회사 주식 현물배당과 자사주 소각을 든다. SK하
이닉스 이사회가 주주영향평가와 보호 방안을 언제, 어떤 내용으로 공시하는지가 확인
할 첫 문서다. 미국 공장의 필요성과 자회사 상장의 필요성은 따로 따질 문제다. 이사회
가 설명할 것은 다른 조달 수단과 견줘 상장이 기존 주주에게 유리한지, 그 근거는 무엇
인가다. 다음 재공시 예정일(12월 3일)을 포함해 확인할 일정은 [표6]에 정리했다. 
결론: 상장 기대보다 
조건 확인이 먼저

SK하이닉스 주가의 큰 방향은 여전히 메모리 업황이 정한다. 솔리다임 상장은 SK하이
닉스 안의 낸드 사업이 어떻게 평가받는지를 정하는 변수다. 그 평가를 가르는 조건은 
세 가지다. 상장 전 출자 단가가 적정한지, 공모 자금이 미국 투자와 모회사 가운데 어
디로 가는지, 투자수익이나 환원 계획이 구체적인지다. SK㈜·SK텔레콤·SK이노베이션은 
NPS 지분의 가치와 남은 출자 부담을 함께 봐야 한다. 상장과 미국 공장은 아직 확정
되지 않았다. 지금은 상장 기대를 미리 더하기보다, 금융위원회의 유권해석과 이사회의 
주주보호 방안, 12월 3일 재공시에서 이 조건을 확인할 때다.

[표6] 확인할 공시와 일정 → 공모가보다 상장 전 단가와 공모 자금의 쓰임이 먼저 
시점 확인할 것 
10 월 28 일 SK 하이닉스 3 분기 실적발표(컨퍼런스콜, 10월 14 일 안내공시): 솔리다임·NPS 관련 질의 여부

이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터
가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적

## Document 308: CEE & CCA week ahead_ A rate decision in Hungary and regional price data

Source PDF: all report pdf/CEE & CCA week ahead_ A rate decision in Hungary and regional price data.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Hungary will announce its latest interest rate decision and
release September wage data next week. Meanwhile, October's
core inﬂation data is due from Poland, alongside producer price
ﬁgures from the Czech Republic

Adam Antoniak, Peter Virovacz and David Havrlant
Budapest, Hungary
Poland: Core inﬂation expected to have
eased below 3% in October
Monday brings the release of October's core inﬂation data in
Poland. On the basis of the available data, we estimate that core
inﬂation excluding food and energy moderated below 3% year-on-
year for the ﬁrst time since March. Despite the surge in fuel prices,
there are few signals of broader inﬂationary pressure in Poland's
economy, even though headline inﬂation is elevated. Still, the
National Bank of Poland is sending hawkish signals and may
tighten monetary policy at the beginning of 2027. We expect rates
to be hiked by 50bp in 1Q27.

### Page 2

Hungary: Market sentiment expected to
keep NBH in wait-and-see mode
Tuesday brings the National Bank of Hungary's latest interest rate
decision. While much of the world is grappling with renewed
inﬂation fears, Hungary remains something of an island of safety,
with both headline and core inﬂation hovering around 2% year-on-
year. That gives the NBH room to ease – but the door remains
locked, and global market sentiment holds the key. With markets

increasingly pricing aggressive rate hikes from major central banks
and market tensions elevated, the NBH has little choice but to
remain in wait-and-see mode. The risk is that by the time the
external backdrop structurally improves, domestic inﬂationary
pressures may already have started to close the window for cuts.
For now, low inﬂation argues for easing, but global markets are
forcing patience. In our base case, we see the base rate remaining

at 5.50% for at least the next three quarters.
Wage data for September will be released on Wednesday. Wage
growth will likely remain elevated for now, but the days of
exceptionally strong real wage growth are numbered. Employers
and employees are negotiating a new minimum wage deal for
2027, as the existing three-year agreement would imply an
unsustainably high 14% increase. We expect the new deal to deliver
a rise of around 5%, setting the stage for a meaningful slowdown in

overall wage growth and easing one of the key sources of domestic
inﬂationary pressure.
Czech Republic: Producer prices expected
to have climbed in October
Czech PPI data will be released on Monday. Producer prices likely
continued their monthly gains in October, which have driven the
annual growth pace higher. Both the surging Brent crude price and
depreciating koruna contributed to rising input costs that were only
partially passed to end prices, putting proﬁtability under extended

pressure.

The Bank Board increased interest rates to 4% in a close call,
and we see valid arguments for both positions. Second-round
eﬀects will push inﬂation above the upper bound of the target
corridor for some time, while dwindling economic activity
warrants lots of caution. We see neither hikes nor cuts as the
most likely outcome for quite some time
David Havrlant
The Czech National Bank's decision to raise rates on 5 November was a close
call
Assessing the impact of Hormuz

The Czech National Bank increased the policy rate by 25bp to 4% in
a split vote on 5 November, with Governor Ales Michl and board
members Jan Kubicek, Jan Prochazka and Jakub Seidler voting for
a hike. Meanwhile, Deputy Governors Eva Zamrazilova and Jan Frait,
along with board member Karina Kubelkova, favoured interest rate
stability. We assess the current situation and the risks to the
outlook as complex and see relevant justiﬁcation for both options,

depending on whether your concerns are more on the side of

### Page 2

second-round inﬂationary eﬀects or on the side of disappointing
economic activity and the consequences for consumption.
The Bank Board assessed overall risks as pro-inﬂationary. There
was an agreement that the secondary impacts of the energy shock
have not been signiﬁcantly reﬂected in domestic inﬂation so far.
However, as the Hormuz conﬂict becomes more protracted, the
likelihood of tangible second-round eﬀects increases. Jakub Seidler
noted that global energy prices have been elevated for too long, so

it would be diﬃcult to avoid a pass-through to other segments of
consumer prices. Deputy Governor Zamrazilova emphasised that
much depends on further developments in the Middle Eastern
conﬂict. Should tensions ease in the near future, a rate hike at the
November meeting would not be necessary. The key question is
whether the conﬂict will eventually pass or become the new
normal, with the latter implying persistently high energy costs.
Deputy Governor Frait stated that energy prices remaining at these

levels for such an extended period could have a negative impact on
demand and economic activity, mainly by reducing the real
incomes of households and companies. The current negative
supply shock has hit the Czech economy from a favourable starting
position, but the factors that helped keep inﬂation close to the
target this year are about to fade. The increase in longer-term rates
contributes to the restrictive stance of monetary policy, and implies

some softening in domestic lending activity. The board members
agreed that this is a key factor reducing the need for further
tightening for the time being.
To rise or not to rise?
However, as Jan Kubicek noted, core inﬂation is not slowing down
as previously assumed and is even slightly increasing. This
represents a fundamental concern, especially in combination with
the disappointing real growth performance. That said, producer
prices have embarked on an upward trend and may put further

pressure on the core rate. Jakub Seidler and Karina Kubelkova
emphasised that the renewed price increases in the energy and
food segment are usually the key drivers when it comes to inﬂation

Producer prices will drive consumer prices
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
En gros, among the main reasons for a rate hike in November were:
i) persistent core inﬂation, ii) still robust wage dynamics and iii)
second-round eﬀects expected to bite soon. Among the main

reasons for staying on hold and evaluating were: i) secondary
inﬂationary impacts are so far limited, ii) long-term rates deliver
restriction, iii) disappointing economic activity.
We see the arguments in both directions as valid, which made the
November decision relatively complex and was reﬂected in the
close vote. Time will tell whether the second-round eﬀects that we
believe are just kicking in will prove signiﬁcant without weighing too
heavily on economic activity. Or whether the deteriorating real

purchasing power and dwindling real wage growth could lead to
weaker household consumption, which would in turn put
downward pressure on inﬂation. Both eﬀects are certainly
interconnected, which only underscores the beauty of
macroeconomics, and the formidable power of prices to clear the
market.

Punchy inﬂation across price segments
When it comes to our inﬂation forecast, we deﬁnitely see pro-
inﬂationary pressures when looking ahead. We expect annual
producer price growth to peak at around 7% in industry early next
year and at around 12% in agriculture mid-next year. At the same
time, the annual pace of import prices already quickened to above
8% in September. Those are not low growth rates, and all are going
to feed into consumer prices sooner or later. At the same time, the

koruna continues to weaken against the dollar, making imports
pricier. With all that on board, we see the yearly average of inﬂation
picking up from 2.1% this year to 3.2% next, reaching a peak of just
below 4% in February.
Inﬂation to hover above the target for
longer

6.5% increase in natural gas prices, an 8% increase in heating
prices and a 6% rise in water charges. Nonetheless, not all
distributors will apply the same increase and not all households will
be aﬀected equally. Based on our best estimate, we expect a 3%
monthly increase in regulated prices in January, resulting in a 3.8%

would propel fertiliser prices and transport costs.
Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline
When it comes to core inﬂation, two opposing forces are set for a
duel: second-round eﬀects, coupled with rising import prices,
against a softening housing market and crumbling real purchasing

power. Timing is of the essence. We expect the former to strike ﬁrst,
but the latter to respond without much hesitation. For now, we see
the forces as roughly balanced, keeping average core inﬂation just
below 3% next year.

wage growth will eventually take its toll. Therefore, we see neither
further hikes nor cuts when looking way ahead, with the 4% policy
rate settling in as the new normal.

## Document 355: Global Markets Outlook 2027_ Growing Pains

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Markets Outlook 2027_ Growing Pains.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

We have published our 2027 Macro Outlook: Steady Baseline, Downside Risks. We flesh 
out the Global Market implications and key asset views here, highlighting 10 
investment themes that are likely to dominate price action in 2027. More detailed 
outlooks for the individual asset classes are forthcoming.  
A maturing market cycle: Stable global growth and declining inflation make for 1.
a friendly macro baseline, but risks are to the downside as the market cycle ages. 
The AI investment impulse peaks: AI has contributed more to market gains 2.
than economic growth, so a bigger test for markets than the economy.  
Passing—or dropping—the baton: Equity gains move from AI infrastructure to 3.
other areas, but we see risks if tighter financial conditions thwart the transition 
or markets look further ahead. 
Energy—balancing risk and risk premium: Our baseline is for a gradual decline 4.
in energy prices, but an escalation-driven spike remains one of the largest 
near-term risks to markets. 
Bonds reclaim macro and hedging value: Higher running yield, more paths to 5.
relief, and cheap valuations argue for higher bond allocations, as supply shocks 
give way to demand stresses. 
A more cyclical Dollar, Asia’s revaluing currencies: Lower inflation and less 6.
tech exceptionalism should make for a softer Dollar through 2027, especially 
versus Asia’s revaluing currencies (JPY, CNY). 
Europe & UK—global shocks and local shifts: European markets are under 7.
pressure from global shocks and local concerns, which may persist until the 
French election, keeping EUR downside attractive. 
The stress of financing the boom: Record credit and equity issuance has been 8.
digestible so far, but with discounted pricing needed in credit, and further 
widening in US credit spreads is likely.

### Page 2

EM—resilience and diversification: The case for EM is exposure to high, diversified 9.
and resilient yields in EM fixed income and a regionally diversified earnings mix in EM
equities.
Growth risks and tail hedges: A benign baseline and tech has de-rated, but 10.
diversification benefits from bonds and commodities, and equity index volatility has
scope to rise further.
12 November 2026   2
Goldman Sachs Global Markets Outlook 2027

### Page 3

Markets Outlook 2027: Growing Pains

1. A maturing market cycle 
Stable growth, declining inflation make for a friendly macro backdrop. n
But risks are to the downside, and an ageing market cycle is facing more headwinds. n
Higher rates, wider spreads, and a peaking AI impulse mean the broad asset picture n
is more two-sided ... 
... and argues for more diversified allocations, including bonds and hedges. n
Our headline macro forecasts for 2027 are quite friendly: stable growth, declining 
inflation and peaking policy rates. But as the economic cycle extends, the market cycle is 
increasingly showing its age. For 2026, our view was that even though asset market 
valuations were running ahead of the macro, a positive cyclical backdrop including 
strong investment growth should dominate. While the Iran war has upended many 
forecasts, even without the energy shock the market is running into more conventional 
late-cycle headwinds. Real rates are higher, credit spreads are wider and volatility is 
picking up, especially under the surface. On top of this, the AI impulse, which has been a 
key support to markets through the shocks this year, looks poised to peak in the coming 
year. 
That is likely to prove a bigger headwind to the profit and asset market picture than 
growth, given its larger role in supporting equity markets. Markets have already moved 
some way towards reflecting that the extraordinary surge in profitability in the AI 
infrastructure complex is unlikely to be extrapolated. So a flattening in the gains there 
may be offset by gains further down the waterfall among AI users or other cyclical parts 
of the market, especially if energy prices and rates fall more. But that is a tricky 
balancing act, and further boosts to equity prices are likely to come alongside a higher 
base level of volatility. There are also risks that AI-related drawdowns tighten financial 
conditions more meaningfully, or that energy and policy relief do not arrive on time. 
That leaves the broader risk-asset picture looking more two-sided than it has for several 
years, arguing for more diversified and better-hedged portfolios. 
12 November 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Global Markets Outlook 2027

### Page 4

2. The AI investment impulse peaks 
The AI investment impulse peak is a bigger test for markets than the economy. n
That impulse has been a larger contributor to growth in market value and earnings n
than in GDP. 
Markets have already de-rated AI infrastructure beneficiaries. n
Valuation excesses are less visible, so the focus is on sustainability of record profits. n
Coming into 2026, we expected continued acceleration in AI investment to dominate 
valuation concerns in equities, but likely amid higher volatility and wider credit spreads.  
Although we expect solid AI investment growth to continue, our forecasts imply that the 
investment growth impulse—additional AI investment as a share of GDP—will peak in 
early 2027. From a macro perspective, this should be a manageable event. But it may be 
a bigger test for markets for three reasons. First, the AI impulse has been a much larger 
contributor to growth in earnings and market value than to growth in GDP. Second, 
AI-related equities have taken credit for significant macro productivity gains, especially 
companies benefiting directly from the infrastructure boom, that have made them more 
vulnerable to any signs that we are on a less optimistic path. Third, the point at which 
growth rates peak (a “second derivative” turning point) often matters, even if underlying 
growth rates (the “first derivative”) remain healthy. Moving through that peak allows 
markets to place limits on the upside of an uncertain distribution and, as growth 
decelerates, the period of positive surprises to expectations often ends too, as we may 
be starting to see for the AI capex cycle. 
Markets have already been reflecting these concerns. Since June, we have seen a 
significant de-rating of companies levered to the AI infrastructure theme, which are 
most sensitive to shifts in the investment outlook and had been the biggest market 
beneficiaries in the first half of 2026. Moreover, robust profits, and a rising share of

### Page 5

income going to listed companies, have helped keep both the imbalances and the deep 
valuation excesses that we saw in the late 1990s at bay. The key question in this cycle is 
whether the market is correctly assessing how much of the recent acceleration in profits, 
which has taken S&P 500 earnings to a record high relative to GDP, will prove persistent, 
especially as the investment cycle itself fades. Without the capital inflows from the EM 
crises in 1997 and 1998—or the rate cuts that goods price deflation allowed during that 
tech cycle—increased financing needs have also been putting clearer upward pressure 
on the cost of capital.

3. Passing—or dropping—the baton 
Strong levels of AI investment growth and profitability should limit the pressure. n
And the baton may be passed from AI infrastructure to other market areas. n
But we see risks of a larger aggregate shock if the market looks forward, or other n
areas cannot take up the slack. 
Tighter financial conditions or credit conditions could create a feedback loop. n
Two things give us comfort in terms of how bumpy this transition through the peak of 
the investment cycle will prove to be. The first is that although the investment impulse 
from AI looks poised to peak, growth in both AI investment and earnings should remain 
firmly positive in 2027, especially earlier in the year. We expect S&P 500 earnings growth 
to decelerate from 36% in 2026 to a still-robust 11% in 2027. This may ultimately help to 
limit the de-rating of even the most directly affected areas and should reduce the 
impact on broader growth-sensitive assets, though it should also imply less relief in core 
rates pricing. The second is that the baton may be passed, at least to a degree, from the 
direct beneficiaries of the AI investment boom to other areas of the market. This 
includes other areas of the AI complex, such as the hyperscalers, if monetization paths 
become more visible; companies that may begin to show benefits from AI productivity 
enhancements; or non-AI companies that see some relief as the crowding out of capital

available to them eases. 
The risk is that what could be more of a rotation—and a flattening in the upward trend in 
asset markets—becomes a bigger aggregate shock. This could happen because the 
market begins to look further forward to the peak in the investment share itself (in 2028 
on our forecasts); because the passing of the baton to other sectors does not take place 
as quickly or as smoothly as needed; or because feedback loops from financial and 
credit conditions accelerate more quickly than in our baseline. We think the risk to 
markets is higher than the risk to the economic picture on each front, because the boom 
itself has had its largest impact there. For that reason, the most likely channel through 
which AI worries could lead to broader economic downside is through financial 
conditions themselves.

4. Energy—balancing risk and risk premium 
An escalation-driven oil spike remains one of the largest near-term risks to markets. n
Our baseline is for a gradual decline in energy prices, but the hedge value of energy n
is still high. 
Downside tail to energy prices with clearer/faster resolution. n
Risk premium has fluctuated, so hedge value also varies over time. n
A potential oil price spike driven by another escalation in the war with Iran remains one 
of the biggest near-term risks to the markets and growth picture. Particularly after the 
fall in oil prices so far in November, pricing is vulnerable to any broadening in the conflict 
and new supply disruptions. We would expect that to hurt equities, support the Dollar 
against European currencies and put upward pressure on rates, all else equal. Europe, 
too, faces a precarious winter, given low storage levels in natural gas which leave the 
region vulnerable to risks from both supply and weather. If those risks are avoided, our 
baseline is for a gradual decline in energy prices over the coming year even without full 
resolution of the conflict. Despite the ongoing war with Iran, oil market supply has 
largely been restored (even if refining capacity is limited), but risks of escalation and 
further disruptions have generally led the market to demand a fluctuating but 
meaningful risk premium above where we think the fundamental supply-demand picture 
would leave them.

### Page 7

Our baseline is a gradual recovery in supply and reduction in energy prices, leaving 
energy and core commodity returns more muted. But the risk of more serious 
disruptions is real, and energy prices could move significantly higher in those scenarios 
at a time when equities and, potentially, bonds could both be under pressure. The 
hedging value of the asset class remains high for that reason. The wrinkle is that the risk 
premium in oil has varied substantially and conditions the value of the hedge. When the 
risk premium falls, as it has done more recently, that hedging value is likely to be higher. 
When that risk premium is very high, as it was at the end of October, oil markets are 
more vulnerable to downside tails if news flow is better than expected, and outright 
longs (rather than options) have generally been more risky. The downside tail from faster 
progress towards resolution than we expect (not just in Iran, but in Ukraine too) is one of 
the clearest sources of upside risks for broader markets. In that scenario, energy prices 
could come down more quickly, supporting both bonds and equities.

5. Bonds reclaim macro and hedging value 
Rates moved up in 2026, on oil, hikes, AI spending and fiscal deficits. n
Central bank pricing now too hawkish relative to our baseline. n
Bonds should be a better hedge as supply shocks give way to demand stresses. n
Higher running yield, more paths to relief, and cheap valuations argue for higher n
bond allocations. 
Interest rates moved higher in 2026 after a low point on the eve of the Iran war. The 
resultant surge in energy prices and central bank pricing has been the primary catalyst 
across most DM and EM rates markets, but real rates, especially in the US, have 
continued to move higher as investment demand from the AI build-out interacts with 
sustained fiscal deficits. Despite that backdrop, we think that central bank pricing in 
many economies, including the US and the UK, is too hawkish relative to what is likely to 
be delivered. Even with the energy shock, the underlying inflation story is still fairly 
benign, which means that policy rates are likely to peak at lower levels relative to 
2023-2024 and reverse earlier. The risk to this view is that the energy shock extends 
further or inflation remains stickier and the labour market proves more resilient, as it did 
this year. 
Bonds have been a poor hedge in this environment of energy supply shocks, rate hikes

### Page 8

and real rate pressures from private and public sector spending. But we think the case 
for duration has shifted in a more constructive direction for several reasons. First, on a 
static basis, bond yields are now providing a good running yield and a decent spread to 
cash. Second, valuations are cheap (we see UST 10y yields at 4.6% by end-2027), 
positioning is underweight, and there are paths to some shallow rate relief from current 
levels under our benign baseline scenario, and scope for larger moves if AI spending 
decelerates more quickly. Third, the increase in yields has started to create stresses that 
indicate that a higher level of real rates may be difficult to sustain. Bonds are already 
starting to illustrate their value as a hedge against shocks of these types—with Bunds 
rallying as French sovereign spreads widened and USTs rallying as tech stocks came 
under pressure. Although energy spikes remain a near-term risk, we are more positive 
about bond allocations even after the recent rally, in part because of that hedging value.

6. A more cyclical Dollar, Asia’s revaluing currencies 
After tight ranges in 2026, lower inflation, energy prices make for a softer Dollar. n
But a bigger USD swing could come if US tech exceptionalism erodes. n
JPY is a more attractive hedge with ‘team Japan’ focused on limiting Yen weakness. n
CNY appreciation has further to run given undervaluation and a surging trade n
surplus.  
The broad Dollar is finishing 2026 on the strong side of tight ranges, buoyed by a sturdy 
US nominal growth backdrop, higher energy prices, and a shift to more hawkish Fed 
pricing. Under our baseline forecast, all three impulses should fade: alongside trend 
growth we expect inflation in the US to converge towards target, energy prices should 
decline as the Middle East disruptions gradually normalize and we expect the Fed to 
move from hikes to cuts as we head into late 2027 and early 2028. That should make for

### Page 9

a softer Dollar backdrop, and we have pencilled in a roughly 3% fall in the broad 
trade-weighted Dollar in 2027. 
But the real swing factor for FX is likely to be how the peak in AI-related tech spending 
affects US equity returns. Over the past several years, tech-related macro and market 
outperformance in the US has sucked in equity capital flows from the rest of the world 
and has been the primary factor supporting the Dollar’s premium valuation. Our 
baseline forecasts envisage a more even distribution of returns globally rather than US 
underperformance. But if US tech performance erodes meaningfully, that would likely 
come alongside some cyclical underperformance of the Dollar rather than a traditional 
‘safe haven’ bid, as we have already seen in short periods over the past couple of years. 
Instead, we would expect the undervalued Yen to prove a better haven in that scenario, 
given its typical sensitivity to equities and rates falling together. With Japanese policy 
authorities also now focused on limiting further Yen depreciation and encouraging 
inflows into Japanese assets, we think that the Yen should increasingly be a more 
attractive hedge to pro-cyclical portfolios. We also see scope for the Yuan to continue to 
appreciate gradually as China’s trade surplus keeps growing, and forward markets 
underestimate how far this process can run given the deep undervaluation starting 
point. While domestic demand is also very weak, we have argued that China needs both 
currency appreciation and expansionary policy, two instruments to hit two targets, to 
move towards better external and internal balance.

7. Europe & UK—global shocks and local shifts 
European markets have been under pressure from energy ToT, higher global yields n
and sovereign stresses. 
Even if global shocks abate, local concerns still persist until the French election, n
keeping EUR downside attractive.

### Page 10

UK equities are resilient and defensive, Sterling has continued to strengthen. n
UK Gilts may be clearer outperformers given their highest beta to oil prices. n
European macro markets have had a tough year, primarily reflecting a negative terms of
trade shock, rate tightening without an offsetting investment spending boom, and most
recently the reappearance of sovereign stresses, especially in France. Even with the
repricing, there are still challenges: the energy risks are likely to be most acute going

through the winter, and the underlying fiscal issues in France are unlikely to be
adequately addressed until the French election in the Spring of 2027. As a result, using
the Euro as a funder for other crosses has been a popular position, and the skew of risks
and low carry continue to make downside optionality attractive for the near term. While
Europe remains hostage to global shocks, the main paths to relief also run through those
global channels—energy relief would be most helpful, as would lower yields in the rest of

core markets. Together they would also lessen the immediate pressure on fiscal budgets,
even though the underlying primary deficit would still need to be addressed. Any
slowdown in AI-related spending may also show European assets in a better relative light
given a much smaller share of tech in the region’s equity indices.
In those scenarios, the UK may shine even brighter. Gilts have had the highest beta to oil
prices, so a reduction in those pressures may provide the most relief to UK rates and to

the fiscal picture there. UK equity indices also have less tech exposure than the US
(although they do embed significant energy exposure), and so may also prove more
defensive in an AI-related spending slowdown and any associated market pressures. And
while the currency may also benefit from a softer Dollar backdrop, it is already the most
overvalued currency within our G10 coverage. Much has gone right for the Pound in
recent months—a smooth transition to a new Labour government, a ‘do-no-harm’

budget delivery, the BoE shifting to hikes, and encouraging talk of a closer settlement
with the EU. But we think further gains are likely to be more challenging, with our
monetary policy forecasts most dovish to market pricing in the UK.
12 November 2026   10
Goldman Sachs Global Markets Outlook 2027

### Page 11

8. The stress of financing the boom 
AI investment financing has driven credit and equity issuance to cycle records. n
This has been digestible so far, but with discounted pricing needed in credit that has n
led spreads wider. 
A further widening in US spreads looks likely and a higher chance of meaningful n
widening than meaningful tightening. 
Europe offers more risk premium; mortgages and structured credit give some refuge n
from corporate supply. 
Credit and equity issuance has been hitting fresh cycle records, and the financing needs 
of the AI investment boom mean that those needs are likely to build further in 2027. So 
far, that issuance has—for the most part—been digestible, albeit with discounted 
pricing, and the credit underperformance that we expected in 2026 because of these 
dynamics has only really become clear in the last few months. Balance sheets of the 
major borrowers are strong and while free cash flow positions for the hyperscalers have 
deteriorated sharply, the overall corporate sector is much more balanced. We expect 
continued bifurcation between AI-related issuers and “everything else”, with episodic 
indigestion of jumbo AI debt financing leading to wider spreads for AI-related sectors.  
We expect further widening in index-level credit spreads in 2027, though in our base 
case the scope is fairly modest. The risks around that view are skewed though. It is hard 
to see scenarios where US credit spreads tighten sharply (there is more scope for 
tightening in Europe where the risk premium has increased more), and easier to see how 
spreads could move wider, particularly if economic conditions deteriorate more than we 
expect or if investors become more uncomfortable about the profitability of the AI 
investment boom. We expect modest US HY underperformance (decompression) in our
